#renderers/xbf/clickActivation.test.js → apps/viewer/src/viewer/clickActivation.test.ts (PORT verbatim)

createClickActivation({commit, doubleClick, defer, cancel}) with fake defer returning ids. Assertions:

  • tap(1,'o1.1',{multiSelect:false}) commits immediately, nothing deferred; tap(0,…) also immediate.
  • tap(1) then tap(2) same target: 1 commit, 1 deferred; double() → true; after running deferred still 1 commit.
  • tap(1,'o1.1'), tap(2,'o1.2',{multiSelect:true}), run → commits ['o1.1','o1.2'], double() true.
  • tap(1), miss(1), tap(2) → 1 commit, double() false. tap(1), miss(2) → double() true.
  • double() consumed: true then false; tap(3) held like a second click; run → 2 commits; double() true.
  • cancel() drops held second click only (1 commit; double() true).
  • doubleClick:false: three taps → 3 commits, nothing deferred.
  • {wait:true}: tap(1) commits nothing, deferred [VIEWER_DOUBLE_CLICK_ACTIVATION_DELAY_MS]; run → 1 commit; double() true. tap(1,wait)+tap(2,wait) → double() false, run → 0 commits. tap(1,wait)+cancel → 0 commits, double() false. doubleClick:false + wait → immediate commit.

    Our use: CadScene click vs double-click "look at face" (see xbfLookAt) must not first select then deselect.

#renderers/xbf/xbfSelectionFilter.test.js → apps/viewer/src/viewer/selection.test.ts (PORT; extract our ViewPage onClick reducer to a pure fn)

nextSelectionIds(selected, picked, additive=false):

  • (['f1'],['f1']) → []; (['f2','f1'],['f1','f2']) → [] (picking exactly the selection, any order, clears it).
  • (['f1'],['f1','f2']) → ['f1','f2']; (['f1','f2','f3'],['f1','f2']) → ['f1','f2'] (otherwise the pick replaces); (['f1'], []) → [].
  • Additive: (['f3'], ['f1','f2'], true) → ['f3','f1','f2']; (['f3','f1','f2'], ['f1','f2'], true) → ['f3']; (['f3'], [], true) → ['f3'].

    Our current reducer (ViewPage.tsx ~L105): plain click on the only selected item clears; plain click elsewhere replaces; shift/ctrl toggles one. Port with refs as ids (${id}#${path}/face ${n}).

#renderers/xbf/xbfChips.test.ts → api.test.ts + SelectionPanel.test.tsx (+ crates/cad-engine/tests/refs.rs)

Original grammar: <file or id>#<selector>, one per line when several are copied; selector is a part label (unique) or an occurrence path, then /face N, /edge N or a named topology. Quoting rule: a label containing /, or one that reads as a topology ref (face 3), is wrapped in double quotes: #"M3/M4 screw"/edge…, #"face 3"/face 3. A label with spaces only is NOT quoted (#L bracket 80 x 60 x 6 mm/face 3). Repeated occurrences get .N suffix (kit/hand/finger.2). Adapted to ours (<id>#<part path>/face <n>, part path = labels joined by /):

  • partRef(id,'gripper/finger_left') = ${id}#gripper/finger_left; faceRef(id,'base',7) = ${id}#base/face 7; edgeRef(id,'base',2) = ${id}#base/edge 2.
  • Multi-selection copy = refs joined by \n in selection order.
  • Round-trip: every copied line parses back (engine inspect with that ref returns the same part/face) — put in crates/cad-engine/tests/refs.rs: build an assembly kit with parts palm, finger ×3 (duplicates), M3/M4 screw, face 3; assert inspect(ref) resolves each, and that duplicate labels get unique paths (docs/api.md says paths are unique — assert the dedupe suffix our engine chooses).
  • Label with spaces: part L bracket 80 x 60 x 6 mm → ${id}#L bracket 80 x 60 x 6 mm/face 3 resolves.
  • If we adopt quoting (cheap feature): ${id}#"M3/M4 screw"/edge 2 and ${id}#"face 3"/face 3 resolve; unquoted M3/M4 screw must not be mis-split.
  • Chip labels (SelectionPanel): single-part doc → face 7 (the page names the part); multi-part → <part> · face 7; two faces on one part → one chip <part> · 2 faces (original compact {name:'arm_1',kind:'face',count:2}); part pick → chip with part label; edge → pin · edge 2.

#renderers/xbf/xbfLookAt.test.js + XbfMeasure.browser.test.mjs → lookAt.test.ts, tests/e2e/measure.spec.ts

Fixture "bracket" (recreate as a model script tests/e2e/fixtures/bracket.ts): plate 40×20×4 centred at origin (z −2..2), Ø8 through hole at origin, two Ø6 pegs at x = ±12 rising to z = 14.

  • Look subject from FaceInfo (via inspect): plane top face → {kind:'plane', normal:[0,0,1], origin:[0,0,2]} (all mesh points z=2); lookAtFor(top, viewDir=[-0.6,-0.6,0.53]) → direction [0,0,1], point [0,0,2] (camera goes to the side the camera was already on).
  • Hole face → {kind:'cylinder', axis:±[0,0,1], origin:[0,0,0], radius:4}.
  • Straight edge along X at y=−10, z=2 → {kind:'line', direction:±[1,0,0], points:[[-18,-10,2],[18,-10,2]]} (the 36 mm comes from the 40 mm edge minus… original fixture has 2 mm corner rounds; with our sharp plate expect [-20,-10,2]..[20,-10,2]).
  • Circle rim → {kind:'circle', axis:±[0,0,1], center:[0,0,2]}; non-planar/non-cylindrical → free; unknown face index → null.
  • e2e: Measure tool, click plate top (z=2) then peg top (z=14) → measure panel shows plane gap 12 mm (original label ⟂ 12.00 mm; ours Plane gap 12 mm via mm()); double-click the plate's front face (normal −Y) from camera at [60,-80,70] → camera ends looking along +Y from −Y side (|eye−target| x and z components < 1e-3·length, y < 0), and only that face remains selected.
  • Original also asserts Ctrl/Cmd+Z removes the measurement and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+Z restores it (only if we add undo).

#renderers/xbf/useXbfScene.test.js + useXbfSceneRecovery.test.tsx → api.test.ts

  • goneFromEngine(err): ApiError status 404 → true; 500 → false; TypeError('Failed to fetch') → false; null → false. Ours: only a 404 on /api/docs/:id switches ViewPage to the not-found page; network errors keep the last mesh and show reconnecting.
  • subscribeEvents reconnect: first WS connection errors → status closed → after backoff reconnects (fake timers) → status open; on reconnect the page refetches the doc once (catch up on missed versions). If the server is gone for good, status stays closed and LiveDot shows it.

#renderers/mesh/MeshRenderer.browser.test.mjs → tests/e2e/open-mesh.spec.ts + crates/cad/tests/server.rs

Fixtures (generate in test): ASCII STL 20 mm cube (solid box, 12 facets, normals 0 0 0); binary STL of the same; binary STL of two bodies (tetrahedron size 10 at x=30 first in file, then 20 mm cube); empty.stl = 84 zero bytes (header + count 0); broken.stl = "solid this is prose and not a mesh\nfacet normal but never a vertex\n"; 3MF of two boxes (base 20 mm at origin colour #D02020, rider 10 mm at x=30 colour #2040D0) — 3MF only if /api/open gains 3MF.

  • Two-body STL → Doc lists 2 parts, largest first (original rows Body 1, Body 2; box = 12 triangles before tetra = 4); clicking each body in the viewer selects that part.

#Cheap features the tests exercise that we lack

  • Label quoting in refs: quote a path segment containing / or matching ^(face|edge) \d+$ (#"M3/M4 screw"/edge 2). Our <part path>/face <n> is ambiguous without it. Engine parser + viewer builders, ~30 lines.
  • Download export from the viewer: GET /api/docs/:id/export?format=step|stl|3mf|glb|obj|dxf|brep streaming the file (Content-Disposition: <model>.<ext>), plus a File ⋯ → Export submenu. Original per-kind menu: model/STEP → STEP, STL, 3MF, GLB (+DXF only when the doc is planar on z=0); STL file → 3MF, GLB; 3MF → STL, GLB; GLB → STL, 3MF. Error text from the engine shown verbatim (e.g. STL needs a solid; this document holds none.).
  • Doc-list thumbnails via the existing snapshot renderer (POST /snapshot → cache PNG per id@version), re-taken on new version only.
  • Persist view state per doc (camera, display mode, clip, hidden parts) in sessionStorage keyed [id], restored on reload (CadViewerFileViews / tabStore semantics: keep only the doc on screen, record version 2, unknown versions → defaults).
  • Fuzzy filter for the parts tree and doc list (fuzzyMatch(needle, hay) → {score, indices}; filename beats dir, shorter path wins) with matched-character highlighting and a hit count.
  • Model switcher in the navbar: 8 most recently updated docs, filterable.
  • Double-click "look at" a face/edge (plane → along normal, cylinder → along axis) using FaceInfo/EdgeInfo from inspect; needs the clickActivation state machine.
  • Open 3MF/GLB files in cad viewer / POST /api/open (mesh import already exists for STL; 3MF is zip+XML).
  • Repeated-part folding in the tree (arm (2)) when sibling labels repeat.
  • Undo/redo of selection/measure (Cmd/Ctrl+Z, Shift+Cmd/Ctrl+Z).
  • Age labels with day step (3d ago) and file size (1.6 MB, 900 B) in DocList.

#JS tests under apps/* and scripts/test (41 files)

What the three apps are:

  • apps/api: api.texttocad.dev, a Vercel function that serves the version feed (latest release on PyPI) and takes in telemetry (PostHog). We have no equivalent and don't plan one, so every row is N/A.
  • apps/mcp: the "MCP Apps" page that an agent host (Codex tabs, inline chat cards) renders in an iframe. It talks to the host over the ui/* JSON-RPC bridge and tunnels HTTP through a cad_http tool call. Our design has no MCP Apps UI: cad mcp is plain stdio with cad_show returning a viewer link. So these are OUT-OF-SCOPE, except the Quick Edit prompt text format.
  • apps/web: the browser host of the shared viewer, served by the engine. This is the closest match to our apps/viewer: URL ↔ document id, history, title, appearance, reconnecting when the engine restarts, clipboard, and copying references. These are the ones worth porting.

None of these files test the MCP JSON-RPC protocol or the server's HTTP routes on the server side. Those tests are Python, under cad_server/mcp and cad_server/viewer. What you see here is only the client side of those routes.

Our viewer as it stands (checked apps/viewer/src):

  • router.ts: navigate() pushes history and does nothing for the same path.
  • api.ts: the token comes from ?token= and is kept in sessionStorage cad.token; requests send a Bearer header; subscribeEvents reconnects the WebSocket with backoff 500 ms → ×2 → 8 s cap; partRef/faceRef/edgeRef build references.
  • format.ts: num/mm/size/basename, and copyText (async clipboard, falling back to execCommand).

#Assertions to port

#apps/mcp/src/host/host.test.ts: Quick Edit prompt text → apps/viewer/src/quickEdit.test.ts

The format the viewer copies or sends for a Quick Edit is the user's text, then a blank line, then context lines:

  • Input: text "Round it.", file /project/parts/a.step, one face reference, and an annotation file.

    Output: "Round it.\n\nFile: /project/parts/a.step\nReferences:\n/project/parts/a.step#base/f:base.extrude1.2\nAnnotation: /tmp/p/a-1.annotation.json".

  • With only a snapshot path, the output is "Round it.\n\nFile: /project/parts/a.step\nSnapshot: /tmp/p/a-1.snapshot.json". A snapshot given inline is appended verbatim instead ("Snapshot JSON:\n{...}").
  • Our adaptation: File: is the doc's script (or file). The References: lines are our ref strings, one per line, in pick order: <id>#<part path>/face <n>, <id>#<part path>/edge <n>, <id>#<part path>. With no picks there is no References: header. The output is always plain text, never an image block.
  • Delivery title (their openai/title): "Quick edit · a.step", i.e. Quick edit · ${basename(file)}. Port it only if we title the prompt.

#apps/web/src/host/adapters.test.mjs: copying references → apps/viewer/src/quickEdit.test.ts

  • Parts [text "Inspect this face", ref] → clipboard gets exactly "Inspect this face\n<ref>" (text parts and refs in order, joined with \n). Result: {status:'copied', partIds:['intro','ref']}.
  • Delivering the same bundle twice (same operationId) returns the same result and writes to the clipboard once.
  • Their file-path form quotes a path that contains spaces: "/Car project/folder/part.step"#bracket/f:.... Ours uses a UUID id, so no quoting is needed. Assert that partRef/faceRef/edgeRef never produce whitespace before #. Part paths may contain spaces, so add a test that a part path with a space is still emitted unquoted after # (the parser on the server splits at the first #).
  • If the clipboard throws Error('Permission denied'), the result is {status:'failed', message:'Permission denied'}, and the next attempt with the same id succeeds and does write. A failure must not be cached as a duplicate.
  • A binary attachment (application/zip), or text plus an image with no ClipboardItem support, returns failed and writes nothing: never a silent subset.

#apps/web/src/host/browserClipboard.test.js → apps/viewer/src/format.test.ts (copyText)

  • With navigator.clipboard.writeText: called once with the text, and no execCommand.
  • When writeText rejects: falls back to a textarea plus document.execCommand('copy'). The textarea holds the text and is selected, and is removed afterwards (net appended count 0). The previous selection range is restored, and the previously focused element gets focus() once.
  • When both fail (no navigator.clipboard, execCommand returns false): theirs rejects with /Clipboard is unavailable/. Ours returns false. Keep the boolean, but assert false and that the textarea is cleaned up.
  • Optional (the pending text case): if the text is a Promise, call navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({'text/plain': promise})]) synchronously inside the gesture (event order item, write). If that throws NotAllowedError, fall back to writeText(awaited).

#apps/web/src/client/workbench/fileParam.test.js → apps/viewer/src/router.test.ts

Adapted to our routes / and /view/:id:

  • docIdFromPath('/view/6f1c2a4e-1b2c-5d3e-8f4a-0123456789ab') → the id. A trailing slash is accepted. '/' → null. '/view/Users/me/a.step' → null.

    They reject an uppercase UUID. Pick a rule and test it; recommended: accept only the lowercase canonical form, since that is how the server prints it.

  • navigate(path) keeps the current location.search, so ?token=… survives navigation. Theirs: from /?debug=1&token=1800000000.mac, writing the id gives /<id>?debug=1&token=1800000000.mac.

    Our navigate currently drops the query. Either assert that the token is in sessionStorage so dropping it is harmless, or make navigate keep it. This test decides which.

  • Navigating to the current path writes nothing (no pushState). This already holds in router.ts.
  • A replace option uses replaceState. Their first write uses replace, later ones push.

#apps/web/src/App.test.mjs → apps/viewer/src/App.test.tsx (+ e2e)

Subset that applies:

  • Loading /view/<ONE> shows doc ONE and adds no history entry on mount (history.length is unchanged).
  • document.title:
    • Once the doc loads, it is the doc's model name (theirs: the file basename one.step, or the envelope name qdd_actuator).
    • Before the doc answers, it keeps the default app title (theirs text-to-cad) and is never the UUID.
    • On the home /, it goes back to the app title.
  • Opening another doc pushes exactly one history entry. Opening the doc already on screen pushes none. popstate back to /view/<ONE> shows ONE. popstate to / shows the doc list.
  • Engine-gone overlay:
    • When a fetch of the doc throws TypeError('Failed to fetch'), exactly one [role="alert"] appears with "CAD is not running." text. The page under it stays mounted but gets inert and aria-hidden="true". The alert never shows the raw error ("Failed to fetch", JSON braces).
    • On a 403 with {error:'engine token required'}, the alert instead reads "This link no longer works. Ask your agent to open the model again and use the new link." Never "engine token required".
    • The first successful read removes the alert and the inert.
    • English strings (theirs): "CAD is not running." + "It starts again when your agent next uses CAD or you open the CAD app; this page reconnects on its own." + a Reload button. Our wording: "start it with cad server start or the next cad run".
  • Copy link (if we add it): copies ${origin}/view/<id>?token=<token>.

#apps/web/src/host/viewerAutoReload.test.js + useViewerAutoReload + useEngineReconnect → apps/viewer/src/reconnect.test.ts

Our analogue: subscribeEvents plus the /api/health identity. This needs the server to expose an instance id: see the cheap features list.

  • Pure state machine next(state, answer, {baseline, now}):
    • Same identity → WATCHING, no reload, next poll at the watch interval.
    • {ok:false} → RELOADING, with since set to the first failure time and kept across later failures.
    • Back with the same identity → WATCHING, no reload (a blip).
    • A different identity that admits our token → reload. There is no upper bound on the wait: a different identity at t = 90 000 ms still reloads.
    • A different identity that refuses our token (403) → FOREIGN: never reload, show "link no longer works", stop polling.
  • Hook timing (theirs, with a fake scheduler): after a loss the delays are [1000, 2000, 4000, 5000] (double, capped at 5 s). Asks always go to the same URL (/api/v1/server → ours /api/health). Exactly one reload once a new instance answers, then polling stops (queue empty).
  • A window focus event asks at once and cancels the pending tick. Unmount cancels the scheduled tick (queue length 0).
  • Answer sequence forbidden, forbidden, down, forbidden, back → the "refused" flag goes true, true, false, true, then exactly one reload, then it stops.
  • Our current WS backoff is 500 ms ×2 capped at 8 s. Test whichever constants we choose, but keep the properties: it doubles, it is capped, and a successful open resets the backoff.

#apps/web/src/host/staleBuild.test.mjs → apps/viewer/src/staleBuild.test.ts

  • First vite:preloadError → reload() once, and sessionStorage STALE_BUILD_KEY = now. A second error in the same page does not reload again.
  • If the stored timestamp is within the window (e.g. now − 1000 ms), no reload. If it is older than the window plus 1 ms, reload.
  • After stop(), events are ignored.

#apps/web/src/host/reloadWhenClean.test.mjs → apps/viewer/src/reloadWhenClean.test.ts

  • pageHasUnsaved(document) is false for a focused empty input, and true once it holds "make the bracket 2 mm thicker".
  • reloadWhenClean(reload) while unsaved: no reload, and a retry is scheduled at RELOAD_RETRY_MS. After the input blurs and the retry fires: exactly one reload, and all listeners removed.
  • Use: our Quick Edit prompt box must not be wiped by a live-reload or reconnect reload.

#apps/web/src/persistence/tabRecord.test.mjs → apps/viewer/src/tabRecord.test.ts

Only if the viewer persists per-tab view state (camera, display mode, panel widths):

  • Constructing the store reads and writes nothing. Defaults: appearance system.
  • After an update, exactly one sessionStorage key exists, holding {version, settings, files}. Per-file entries are keyed by JSON.stringify([path, kind]) (ours: doc id).
  • The same storage reloaded gives an identical snapshot. A fresh storage gives the defaults.
  • If getItem/setItem throw: defaults, writes don't throw, and state is kept in memory. Junk ('{not json') or version: 0 gives the defaults.
  • Our token is already in sessionStorage cad.token: decide whether it moves into the record.

#apps/web/src/client/ui/colorScheme.test.js → apps/viewer/src/colorScheme.test.ts

  • Options ['system','light','dark']. normalize(' Dark ') → dark. 'cinematic', '', null, undefined, 7 → system.
  • resolve('system', {prefersDark:true}) → dark. resolve('light', {prefersDark:true}) → light. resolve('dark', {prefersDark:false}) → dark.
  • Applying a scheme sets documentElement.classList dark and style.colorScheme. An unknown id resolves as system. A null root doesn't throw.
  • First-paint inline <script> in the <head> before </head>: reads the stored record and paints dark without a flash. Blocked storage still paints (light when the OS is light).

#apps/web/src/shell.test.mjs (+ apps/mcp build.test notranslate) → apps/viewer/src/indexHtml.test.ts

  • apps/viewer/index.html: the <html> tag has translate="no" and class="notranslate", and <meta name="google" content="notranslate"> is present. Browser translation rewrites the text nodes React owns and crashes the next commit.

#apps/web/scripts/viteConfig.test.mjs → apps/viewer/vite.config.test.ts

  • The Vite config (build or serve) has server.cors === false.
  • envPrefix is a viewer-only prefix (theirs VIEWER_), so CAD_TOKEN and CAD_SERVER_URL don't start with it. Loading the config with VIEWER_*TOKEN* set throws, with a message saying Vite hands every prefixed variable to the page.
  • If our dev server proxies /api to cad server with a token, the token must be injected by the proxy, never through import.meta.env.

#apps/web/scripts/selfContained.test.mjs → apps/viewer/test/selfContained.test.ts

  • No relative import specifier (from '…', import('…'), require('…')) in apps/viewer/**/*.{ts,tsx,js,mjs} resolves outside apps/viewer. Shared code comes only through package names (@cad/sdk).
  • Every inline markdown link [x](rel) in apps/viewer/**/*.md resolves to an existing file inside the repo. URLs, anchors and mailto are skipped.
  • No package.json script contains ../.
  • Skip these dirs: node_modules, dist, .vite, coverage.

#apps/web/src/host/cadClient.test.mjs → apps/viewer/src/source/http.test.ts

  • Fetching a doc from /view/<id> requests /api/docs/<id> at the origin root. It is never resolved relative to the page path, so never /view/api/.... Today api.ts uses absolute paths: keep a test.
  • Adaptation of "nothing asked while hidden": when the page becomes visible again (visibilitychange → visible), refetch the doc list or the current doc if a doc event arrived while hidden, or if the WS reconnected. Theirs: 2 reads in 2 s while visible, 0 more over 10 s hidden, 1 read on becoming visible, and the new revision is shown.

#Cheap features the tests exercise that we lack

  • Server instance id in /api/health (e.g. instance: <uuid per process>). With it the viewer can tell three cases apart: a blip (same instance), a restart (new instance, same token: reload or refetch), and a foreign or rotated server (403 → "link no longer works"). Today a 403 after a restart is just an error.
  • Engine-gone overlay: one role="alert" over an inert page, with friendly text for "not running" versus "token refused", cleared on the first successful read.
  • Quick Edit prompt format: text\n\nFile: <script>\nReferences:\n<ref>…, plus duplicate-delivery suppression, so a double-click doesn't double-copy.
  • Keep ?token= across in-app navigation, or make a deliberate choice to rely on sessionStorage.
  • Don't reload while the Quick Edit box has text (reloadWhenClean), and reload once on vite:preloadError (stale chunks after cad is upgraded while a tab is open).
  • notranslate on index.html: a one-line fix for React crashes under Chrome's page translation.
  • Theme setting (System/Light/Dark) with a no-flash first-paint script.
  • Document title = model name (never the id); the app title on the home page.
  • Browser download of exports: a GET /api/docs/:id/export?format=step returning the file (Content-Disposition), clicked through a same-origin <a download>. Today export only writes to absolute server paths.
  • Copy link in the viewer: ${origin}/view/<id>?token=<token>.
